AgentHPOBench：评估 LLM 代理作为顺序超参数优化器的基准AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers

怀天宇Tianyu Huai2,_3,4 、范廷硕, Tingshuo Fan1,4 , 陈新驰, Xinchi Chen1,4,† , 郑伊宁, Yining Zheng1,2,4 , 王雨欣, Yuxin Wang1,2,4 , 陈爽, Shuang Chen1,2,4 , 周杰, Jie Zhou3 , 黄玄晶, Xuanjing Huang1,_2

1复旦大学 2上海创新研究院 3华东师范大学 4OpenMOSS1Fudan University 2Shanghai Innovation Institute 3East China Normal University 4OpenMOSS

摘要Abstract

随着法学硕士从代码完成系统发展成为自主科学代理，评估他们进行实验的能力变得越来越重要。现有的基准通常侧重于静态代码生成、论文复制或最终答案的正确性，但不直接评估智能体是否可以解释实验证据并使用它来指导后续的超参数决策。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 AgentHPOBench，这是一个连续基准测试，包含 7 个研究类别的 30 个可执行机器学习任务。每个任务都从经过验证的基线运行开始，之后代理执行多次连续干预。在每个步骤中，代理都会观察累积的配置、指标和日志，然后再提出下一个有效配置。我们在统一协议下评估 12 种广泛使用的药物和传统 HPO 基线。结果表明，当前的代理在跨领域表现出可测量的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代细化、复杂日志诊断以及报告参考性能的一致进展方面仍然面临明显的局限性。As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

GitHub：GitHub: https://github.com/OpenMOSS/AgentHPOBench

1 简介1 Introduction

随着法学硕士在推理、长上下文的使用和工具交互方面的进步，代理人正变得有能力完成日益复杂的工作流程，这些工作流程构成了实证研究的一部分。因此，最近的基准测试已经超越了静态问答和独立代码生成，转向了可执行的研究环境。 MLGym [1] 和 MLE-Dojo [2] 已经提供了交互式环境，代理可以在其中执行实验并通过反馈完善解决方案。 PaperBench [3]、AutoExperiment [4]、RE-Bench [5]、MLR-Bench [6] 和 AIRS-Bench [7] 进一步将评估扩展到论文复制、研究工程和 ML 研究生命周期的更广泛阶段。这些基准展示了迭代评估的价值，但它们通常评估广泛的研究或工程工作流程，其中的改进可能来自数据处理、代码修改、架构设计、调试、超参数调整或这些操作的组合。As LLMs improve in reasoning, use of long contexts, and tool interaction, agents are becoming capable of completing increasingly complex workflows that form part of empirical research. Recent benchmarks have therefore moved beyond static question answering and isolated code generation toward executable research environments. MLGym [1] and MLE-Dojo [2] already provide interactive environments in which agents execute experiments and refine solutions through feedback. PaperBench [3], AutoExperiment [4], RE-Bench [5], MLR-Bench [6], and AIRS-Bench [7] further extend evaluation toward paper replication, research engineering, and broader stages of the ML research lifecycle. These benchmarks demonstrate the value of iterative evaluation, but they generally assess broad research or engineering workflows in which improvements may arise from data processing, code modification, architecture design, debugging, hyperparameter tuning, or combinations of these actions.

传统的 HPO 基准测试解决了问题的不同方面。它们提供受控的表格、代理或可执行目标来比较优化算法 [8, 9]，但通常会抽象出研究存储库的日志、配置和程序上下文。这留下了特定的能力Traditional HPO benchmarks address a different aspect of the problem. They provide controlled tabular, surrogate, or executable objectives for comparing optimization algorithms [8, 9], but typically abstract away the logs, configurations, and procedural context of research repositories. This leaves a specific capability

未充分检查：代理是否可以解释已完成的存储库实验中的证据并将其转换为下一个有效的超参数配置。在典型的机器学习工作流程中，研究人员执行基线，检查目标和辅助指标以及执行日志，并决定在下一个实验中调整哪些参数。因此，我们提出一个重点问题：自主代理能否通过经验反馈引导的一系列超参数干预来改进真实的机器学习实验？insufficiently examined: whether an agent can interpret evidence from completed repository experiments and convert it into the next effective hyperparameter configuration. In a typical ML workflow, researchers execute a baseline, inspect target and auxiliary metrics together with execution logs, and decide which parameters to adjust in the next experiment. We therefore ask a focused question: can an autonomous agent improve real ML experiments through a sequence of hyperparameter interventions guided by empirical feedback?

为了解决这个问题，我们引入To address this question, we introduceAgentHPOBench是一个专用的受控基准，用于评估可执行研究存储库中的代理的顺序 HPO，如图 1 所示。AgentHPOBench 包含从最近的 ML 研究存储库构建的 30 个可执行任务，涉及七个类别：自然语言处理、计算机视觉、时间序列预测、图学习、强化学习、大语言建模和结构化学习。通过关注最新的研究存储库，该任务套件捕获了通常被传统 HPO 基准抽象出来的当代训练管道、配置接口和评估实践。表 1 总结了 AgentHPOBench 和代表性 HPO 基准测试之间的差异。, a dedicated and controlled benchmark for evaluating sequential HPO by agents in executable research repositories, as shown in Figure 1. AgentHPOBench contains 30 executable tasks constructed from recent ML research repositories across seven categories: natural language processing, computer vision, time series forecasting, graph learning, reinforcement learning, large language modeling, and structured learning. By focusing on recent research repositories, the task suite captures contemporary training pipelines, configuration interfaces, and evaluation practices that are often abstracted away by conventional HPO benchmarks. Table 1 summarizes the differences between AgentHPOBench and representative HPO benchmarks.

AgentHPOBench 由三个组件组成。首先，任务构建协议将每个存储库映射到具有参考基线、目标度量、受限干预空间以及论文或存储库锚点的可执行优化任务。其次，统一执行工具验证建议的配置、执行实验并记录配置、指标、日志和决策的完整序列。第三，评分和审核层验证任务轨迹，并将异构任务结果转换为平均有界归一化分数、基线获胜率和平均锚点获得率。这些指标将参考基线的相对改进与报告绩效的绝对实现区分开来。这些组件共同实现了一个通用的顺序评估协议。每项任务都以经过验证的参考基线开始，该参考基线由在相同预算设置下评估的所有代理和方法共享。基线运行后，代理会收到固定数量的干预机会。每次干预时，AgentHPOBench comprises three components. First, the task construction protocol maps each repository to an executable optimization task with a reference baseline, a target metric, a constrained intervention space, and a paper or repository anchor. Second, the unified execution harness validates proposed configurations, executes experiments, and records the complete sequence of configurations, metrics, logs, and decisions. Third, the scoring and auditing layer verifies task traces and converts heterogeneous task results into mean bounded normalized score, baseline win rate, and mean anchor attainment. These metrics distinguish relative improvement over the reference baseline from absolute attainment of reported performance. Together, these components implement a common sequential evaluation protocol. Each task begins with a validated reference baseline that is shared by all agents and methods evaluated under the same budget setting. After the baseline run, the agent receives a fixed number of intervention opportunities. At each intervention, it

2

观察累积的配置、目标和辅助指标以及执行日志，并在源自官方培训脚本、配置文件或存储库文档的干预空间内提出新的配置。数据集、数据分割、目标指标、评估代码和基准元数据保持固定。最终干预后的审核结果用于评分。因此，AgentHPOBench 隔离了将实验反馈转换为下一个有效配置的能力。observes the accumulated configurations, target and auxiliary metrics, and execution logs, and proposes a new configuration within the intervention space derived from the official training scripts, configuration files, or repository documentation. The dataset, data split, target metric, evaluation code, and benchmark metadata remain fixed. The audited result after the final intervention is used for scoring. AgentHPOBench therefore isolates the ability to convert experimental feedback into the next valid configuration.

我们在相同的基线和干预机会数量下评估具有开放权重和 API 代理的模型以及传统的 HPO 方法。结果表明，当前的代理可以改善参考基线，并且一些代理获得比传统 HPO 方法更强的聚合结果。然而，这种优势在不同的任务类别或评估设置中并不一致。全额训练预算下的评估提高了平均有界归一化分数和平均锚点获得率，但并没有统一提高基线获胜率。反馈消融表明，删除中间实验证据会降低优化性能，而轨迹分析表明，后来的干预措施可能会趋于稳定或放弃早期的成果。这些发现表明，发现有用的配置并通过反馈可靠地改进它是不同的能力。We evaluate models with open weights and API agents, together with conventional HPO methods under the same baseline and number of intervention opportunities. The results show that current agents can improve reference baselines and that several agents obtain stronger aggregate results than conventional HPO methods. However, this advantage is not consistent across task categories or evaluation settings. Evaluation under the full training budget improves both mean bounded normalized score and mean anchor attainment, but does not uniformly increase baseline win rate. The feedback ablation shows that removing intermediate experimental evidence reduces optimization performance, while trajectory analysis reveals that later interventions may plateau or discard earlier gains. These findings show that discovering a useful configuration and reliably refining it through feedback are distinct capabilities.

简而言之，我们的贡献如下：In a nutshell, our contributions are as follows:

  • 我们引入 AgentHPOBench 来评估代理是否可以将实验反馈转换为可执行研究存储库中的有效超参数决策。该基准包含来自最新 ML 存储库的 7 个研究类别的 30 项任务。We introduce AgentHPOBench for evaluating whether agents can convert experimental feedback into effective hyperparameter decisions in executable research repositories. The benchmark contains 30 tasks from recent ML repositories across seven research categories.

  • 我们开发了一个统一的评估框架，其中包含干预空间和执行工具，可在共享参考基线和固定任务定义下验证、执行和记录代理干预。该框架既衡量基线的改善情况，又衡量报告参考绩效的实现情况。We develop a unified evaluation framework with intervention spaces and an execution harness that validates, executes, and records agent interventions under shared reference baselines and fixed task definitions. The framework measures both improvement over the baseline and attainment of reported reference performance.

  • 我们在 AgentHPOBench 上使用传统的 HPO 方法评估了多种代理。结果提供了基于存储库的 HPO 中当前代理的功能和局限性的系统视图。We evaluate several agents together with conventional HPO methods on AgentHPOBench. The results provide a systematic view of the capabilities and limitations of current agents in repository based HPO.

2 相关工作2 Related Work

语言代理的基准。Benchmarks for language agents.越来越多的工作评估机器学习研究工作流程中的代理。 MLAgentBench [14]、ML-Bench [15] 和 MLE-bench [16] 检查代码库使用、模型训练和 ML 工程，而 CORE-Bench [17]、RE-Bench [5] 和 PaperBench [3] 则专注于可重复性、研究工程和论文复制。 MLGym [1]、MLE-Dojo [2]、MLR-Bench [6] 和 AIRS-Bench [7] 将评估范围扩大到更完整的 ML 研究工作流程。这些基准评估一般研究执行或复制，而 AgentHPOBench 隔离代理是否可以将存储库指标和日志转换为下一个有效的超参数配置。A growing body of work evaluates agents in ML research workflows. MLAgentBench [14], ML-Bench [15], and MLE-bench [16] examine codebase use, model training, and ML engineering, while CORE-Bench [17], RE-Bench [5], and PaperBench [3] focus on reproducibility, research engineering, and paper replication. MLGym [1], MLE-Dojo [2], MLR-Bench [6], and AIRS-Bench [7] broaden evaluation to more complete ML research workflows. These benchmarks assess general research execution or reproduction, whereas AgentHPOBench isolates whether agents can convert repository metrics and logs into the next valid hyperparameter configuration.

HPO 方法和基准。HPO methods and benchmarks.OpenML 基准测试套件 [18] 和 HPOBench [8] 提供用于比较优化算法的标准化任务，而 NAS-Bench-101 [19] 为架构搜索提供可重用的目标。常见的 HPO 方法包括随机搜索 [20]、使用代理模型的方法 [21-23]、Hyperband [24] 和 BOHB [25] 等资源分配方法以及基于群体的训练 [26]。这些方法和基准通常假设预定义的搜索空间、结构化的目标界面和相对干净的数值反馈。OpenML benchmark suites [18] and HPOBench [8] provide standardized tasks for comparing optimization algorithms, while NAS-Bench-101 [19] provides reusable objectives for architecture search. Common HPO approaches include random search [20], methods that use surrogate models [21–23], resource allocation methods such as Hyperband [24] and BOHB [25], and Population Based Training [26]. These methods and benchmarks generally assume a predefined search space, a structured objective interface, and relatively clean numerical feedback.

法学硕士和优化代理。LLMs and agents for optimization.最近的工作还使用语言模型作为优化器或 HPO 助手。 OptFormer 通过调整轨迹学习优化器 [27]，LLAMBO 将 LLM 纳入贝叶斯优化 [28]，其他研究研究由 LLM 和基于代理的优化框架指导的 HPO 决策 [29-31]。 AgentHPOBench 没有提出另一种优化方法，而是评估代理如何可靠地将来自可执行研究存储库的累积反馈转换为有效的配置和经验改进。Recent work also uses language models as optimizers or HPO assistants. OptFormer learns an optimizer from tuning traces [27], LLAMBO incorporates LLMs into Bayesian optimization [28], and other studies investigate HPO decisions guided by LLMs and optimization frameworks based on agents [29–31]. Rather than proposing another optimization method, AgentHPOBench evaluates how reliably agents convert accumulated feedback from executable research repositories into valid configurations and empirical improvement.

图2Figure 2AgentHPOBench 概述。每个任务都是从可执行的 ML 存储库构建的，具有参考基线、目标指标、干预空间、锚点和标准化任务接口。在每一步中，代理都会观察累积的指标和日志，并为下一次运行提出有效的配置。该工具验证并执行建议，记录结果跟踪，并在评分前审核完整的轨迹。Overview of AgentHPOBench. Each task is constructed from an executable ML repository with a reference baseline, target metric, intervention space, anchor, and standardized task interface. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated metrics and logs and proposes a valid configuration for the next run. The harness validates and executes the proposal, records the resulting trace, and audits the completed trajectory before scoring.

3 方法3 Method

我们引入了 AgentHPOBench，这是一个基准和评估工具，用于评估代理是否可以通过顺序超参数干预来改进可执行的 ML 实验，如图 2 所示。与评估跨多个步骤、代码修改或论文复制的一般执行的代理基准不同，AgentHPOBench 隔离了特定的实验功能：将已执行的存储库实验的日志和指标转换为下一次运行的有效配置。代理仅从预定义的干预空间中选择值，而线束验证并执行建议的配置并返回结果观察结果。重复此过程预定数量的干预步骤。表 1 将 AgentHPOBench 与代表性 HPO 基准进行了比较。大多数先前的 HPO 基准测试都提供受控的表格、代理或包装目标来比较优化算法，但抽象了存储库执行细节、文本日志和顺序交互。相反，AgentHPOBench 将真实的研究存储库公开为可执行任务单元，并使用与相应论文或存储库报告的结果相关的任务指标来评估顺序优化。We introduce AgentHPOBench, a benchmark and evaluation harness for assessing whether agents can improve executable ML experiments through sequential hyperparameter interventions, as shown in Figure 2. Unlike agent benchmarks that evaluate general execution across multiple steps, code modification, or paper reproduction, AgentHPOBench isolates a specific experimental capability: converting the logs and metrics of an executed repository experiment into a valid configuration for the next run. The agent selects values only from the predefined intervention space, while the harness validates and executes the proposed configuration and returns the resulting observations. This process is repeated for a predefined number of intervention steps. Table 1 compares AgentHPOBench with representative HPO benchmarks. Most prior HPO benchmarks provide controlled tabular, surrogate, or wrapped objectives for comparing optimization algorithms, but abstract away repository execution details, textual logs, and sequential interaction. AgentHPOBench instead exposes real research repositories as executable task units and evaluates sequential optimization using task metrics tied to results reported by the corresponding papers or repositories.

3.1 AgentHPOBench

AgentHPOBench 由 30 个任务组成，这些任务由 GitHub 上的 30 个可执行 ML 存储库构建。任务构建遵循三个原则。首先，每项任务都必须需要在可执行的培训或评估流程中做出实质性的实验决策。其次，每项任务必须在每次干预后提供可测量的反馈，从而允许基准评估代理如何使用先前的结果来为后续决策提供信息。第三，每项任务必须尽可能保留原始存储库的脚本、依赖项、日志和故障模式，以保持接近真实的研究实践。AgentHPOBench consists of 30 tasks constructed from 30 executable ML repositories on GitHub. Task construction follows three principles. First, each task must require a substantive experimental decision within an executable training or evaluation pipeline. Second, each task must provide measurable feedback after every intervention, allowing the benchmark to evaluate how the agent uses previous outcomes to inform subsequent decisions. Third, each task must remain close to real research practice by preserving the scripts, dependencies, logs, and failure modes of the original repository whenever possible.

每个任务都基于原始存储库或其官方复制环境中的可执行实验。可执行任务接口指定工具如何启动实验、提取目标指标以及根据标准化结果协议记录输出。对于每项任务，我们根据官方训练脚本、配置文件或存储库文档公开的超参数和有效值构建干预空间。我们仅保留影响执行或结果的字段Each task is based on an executable experiment from the original repository or its official reproduction environment. An executable task interface specifies how the harness launches the experiment, extracts the target metric, and records the outputs under a standardized result protocol. For each task, we construct the intervention space from the hyperparameters and valid values exposed by the official training scripts, configuration files, or repository documentation. We retain only fields that affect the execution or outcome of

4

实验。代理只能修改这些预定义字段，而数据集、数据分割、目标指标、评估代码和基准元数据保持固定。the experiment. Agents may modify only these predefined fields, while the dataset, data split, target metric, evaluation code, and benchmark metadata remain fixed.

3.2 问题表述3.2 Problem Formulation

我们将可执行研究存储库的自主超参数优化制定为顺序决策问题。每个任务We formulate autonomous hyperparameter optimization over executable research repositories as a sequential decision problem. Each task𝑡由存储库特定实验目标、受限干预空间 Ω 定义is defined by a repository specific experimental objective, a constrained intervention space Ω𝑡、评估协议和标量性能指标。对于任务, an evaluation protocol, and a scalar performance metric. For task𝑡低于预算设定under budget setting𝑟, 让, let𝑥𝑡,0 ε Ω0 ∈ Ω𝑡表示参考基线配置。该配置是在任务构建期间选择和验证的，并由所有代理和传统优化器共享。执行中denote the reference baseline configuration. This configuration is selected and validated during task construction and is shared by all agents and conventional optimizers. Executing𝑥𝑡,0 下0 under𝑟产生参考基线性能produces the reference baseline performance

之后After𝐾顺序干预，这个过程产生轨迹sequential interventions, this process produces the trajectory

该公式在两个方面与传统的 HPO 基准不同。首先，目标嵌入在可执行的研究存储库中，而不是通过干净的目标接口公开。其次，代理必须解释实验证据，包括日志、指标、任务约束和先前的执行结果，并将这些证据转换为下一个实验的有效配置。This formulation differs from conventional HPO benchmarks in two respects. First, the objective is embedded in an executable research repository rather than exposed through a clean objective interface. Second, the agent must interpret experimental evidence, including logs, metrics, task constraints, and prior execution outcomes, and convert this evidence into a valid configuration for the next experiment.

3.3 评估工具3.3 Evaluation Harness

为了使基准在代理之间可执行且具有可比性，我们实施了统一的评估工具。该工具标准化了代理和异构研究存储库之间的交互，同时保留了每个任务的原始执行逻辑。它启动实验，向代理提供任务上下文，验证建议的配置，并在标准化结果模式下记录结果输出。To make the benchmark executable and comparable across agents, we implement a unified evaluation harness. The harness standardizes the interaction between agents and heterogeneous research repositories while preserving the original execution logic of each task. It launches experiments, provides task context to the agent, validates proposed configurations, and records the resulting outputs under a standardized result schema.

对于每个任务，该工具都会维护代理可见跟踪以及包含建议配置、提取指标、执行日志、运行时元数据和完成情况的内部执行记录For each task, the harness maintains the agent visible trace, together with an internal execution record containing the proposed configurations, extracted metrics, execution logs, runtime metadata, and completion

该工具还增强了具有开放权重的模型和 API 代理之间的可比性。所有代理都使用相同的任务定义、预算设置、干预次数、允许的干预空间、评分规则和结果模式进行评估。不同的后端通过每个后端特定的接口接收相同的任务信息。它们的输出被解析为通用配置模式并针对 Ω 进行验证The harness also enforces comparability between models with open weights and API agents. All agents are evaluated using the same task definitions, budget setting, number of interventions, allowed intervention space, scoring rules, and result schema. The different backends receive the same task information through interfaces specific to each backend. Their outputs are parsed into a common configuration schema and validated against Ω𝑡执行前。before execution.

在评分之前，线束会审核所有任务记录。审核验证基线和所需干预记录的存在、是否符合结果模式、正确的指标提取以及与参考基线和锚点的一致性。只有通过这些检查的完整记录才会包含在汇总评分中。Before scoring, the harness audits all task records. The audit verifies the presence of the baseline and the required intervention records, compliance with the result schema, correct metric extraction, and consistency with the reference baseline and anchor. Only complete records that pass these checks are included in aggregate scoring.

3.4 评分3.4 Scoring

由于基准测试任务使用异构指标，因此我们使用最终干预后的审核结果来评估每个任务。对于任务Because the benchmark tasks use heterogeneous metrics, we evaluate each task using the audited result after the final intervention. For task𝑡低于预算设定under budget setting𝑟, 让, let𝑏𝑡，𝑟𝑏𝑡,𝑟 = 𝑦𝑡,0表示参考基线，0 denote the reference baseline,𝑠𝑡,𝑟 = 𝑦𝑡，𝐾𝑦𝑡,𝐾表示最终结果，并且denote the final result, and𝑎𝑡表示存储库锚点。在评分之前，这些值以相同的数值范围表示并定向，以便较大的值表示更好的性能。我们将定向值表示为denote the repository anchor. Before scoring, these values are expressed on the same numerical scale and oriented so that larger values indicate better performance. We denote the oriented values by𝑏˜ 𝑡,𝑟 , 𝑠 ˜ 𝑡,𝑟, 和, and𝑎 ˜ 𝑡 .

我们使用平均有界归一化分数和基线获胜率作为主要指标。标准化分数是We use mean bounded normalized score and baseline win rate as the primary metrics. The normalized score is

其中审计确保分母为正且非零。减少任务的影响where the audit ensures that the denominator is positive and nonzero. To reduce the influence of tasks with a

平均有界归一化分数 (MBNS) 为The mean bounded normalized score (MBNS) is

哪里where𝑇是评估任务的数量。有界分数 0 与基线匹配，1 达到或超过锚点，负值表示退化。is the number of evaluated tasks. A bounded score of 0 matches the baseline, 1 reaches or exceeds the anchor, and a negative value indicates degradation.

基线胜率（BWR）定义为The baseline win rate (BWR) is defined as

它衡量最终结果优于参考基线的任务比例。It measures the proportion of tasks for which the final result improves over the reference baseline.

作为次要指标，我们报告原始指标方向的锚点实现情况：As a secondary metric, we report anchor attainment in the original metric direction:

平均锚定达到 (MAA) 定义为Mean anchor attainment (MAA) is defined as

锚点达到 100% 表示最终结果与存储库锚点匹配，而以上值表示超出锚点。An anchor attainment of 100% indicates that the final result matches the repository anchor, while values above indicate that it exceeds the anchor.

4 实验4 Experiments

实验设置和指标。Experimental Setup and Metrics.我们在 AgentHPOBench 上评估代理的七个类别：NLP、CV、TS、Graph、RL、LLM 和 SL。除非另有说明，我们使用有限预算协议。线束首先执行参考基线，然后请求代理进行五次连续干预。在此协议下，基线和每次干预均使用相应原始实验训练预算的约 10%。这种设置保留了每项任务的可执行训练和经验反馈，同时使跨存储库和代理的广泛评估在计算上可行。在每次干预之前，代理都会接收累积的跟踪，包括以前的配置、目标和辅助指标以及执行日志。所有任务分数均使用最终干预后的结果，而不是最佳中间结果。我们报告平均有界归一化得分（MBNS）、基线获胜率（BWR）和平均锚点达到（MAA）作为指标。类别分数是每个类别内任务的平均值，而总分数是直接计算所有 30 个任务的分数，每个任务的权重相等。We evaluate agents on AgentHPOBench across seven categories: NLP, CV, TS, Graph, RL, LLM, and SL. Unless otherwise stated, we use the limited budget protocol. The harness first executes the reference baseline and then requests five sequential interventions from the agent. Under this protocol, both the baseline and each intervention use approximately 10% of the training budget of the corresponding original experiment. This setting retains executable training and empirical feedback for each task while making broad evaluation across repositories and agents computationally feasible. Before each intervention, the agent receives the accumulated trace, including previous configurations, target and auxiliary metrics, and execution logs. All task scores use the result after the final intervention rather than the best intermediate result. We report mean bounded normalized score (MBNS), baseline win rate (BWR), and mean anchor attainment (MAA) as the metrics. Category scores average the tasks within each category, whereas overall scores are computed directly over all 30 tasks, giving each task equal weight.

代理和实施细节。Agents and Implementation Details.我们使用开放权重和 API 代理来评估这两个模型。具有开放权重的模型包括 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-32B [32]、Gemma2-2B [33]、DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B [34]、Phi-4-14B [35] 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct [36]。 API 代理包括 DeepSeek-V4-Pro、GPT-5.5、GLM-4.7、GLM-5.1、Kimi-2.6 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6。我们另外将这些代理与随机搜索 [20]、TPE [22] 和 BOHB 式方法 [25] 作为传统 HPO 基线进行比较。所有代理和 HPO 基线使用相同的任务定义、参考基线、干预空间、配置评估数量、预算We evaluate both models with open weights and API agents. The models with open weights include Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B [32], Gemma2-2B [33], DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B [34], Phi-4-14B [35], and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct [36]. The API agents include DeepSeek-V4-Pro, GPT-5.5, GLM-4.7, GLM-5.1, Kimi-2.6, and Claude Sonnet 4.6. We additionally compare these agents with random search [20], TPE [22], and a BOHB-style method [25] as conventional HPO baselines. All agents and HPO baselines use the same task definitions, reference baselines, intervention spaces, number of configuration evaluations, budget

设置和评分规则。传统的 HPO 基线在先前评估的配置和目标指标值上运行，而代理另外接收任务上下文、辅助指标和执行日志。我们使用三个实验种子重复所有本地可执行的开放权重代理和传统 HPO 基线，{0settings, and scoring rules. The conventional HPO baselines operate on previously evaluated configurations and target metric values, whereas agents additionally receive task context, auxiliary metrics, and execution logs. We repeat all locally executable open-weight agents and conventional HPO baselines using three experiment seeds, {0, 1 ,42}。对于这些方法，可以显式控制实现、模型检查点或优化算法、运行时环境和随机状态，从而允许重复测量对基准随机性的敏感性。 API 代理在规范基准实例上评估一次。附录中提供了更多详细信息。42}. For these methods, the implementation, model checkpoint or optimization algorithm, runtime environment, and random state can be explicitly controlled, allowing the repetitions to measure sensitivity to benchmark stochasticity. API agents are evaluated once on the canonical benchmark instance. Further details are provided in Appendix.

主要结果。Main Results.表 2 总结了有限预算协议下的结果。 Claude Sonnet 4.6 实现了最高的总体 MBNS 0.407、最高的 BWR 76.7% 和最高的 MAA 79.5%。在开放重量制剂中，Qwen3-32B 获得最高的总体 MBNS (0.148) 和 MAA (69.1%)，而 Phi-4-14B 获得最高的 BWR (63.3%)。在相同的五个干预机会下，传统的 HPO 方法通常在整体表现上落后于较强的代理人。在这些基线中，BOHB 变体获得了最高的总体 MBNS (0.018) 和 MAA (65.3%)，而随机搜索获得了最高的 BWR (48.9%)。Table 2 summarizes the results under the limited-budget protocol. Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves the highest overall MBNS of 0.407, the highest BWR of 76.7%, and the highest MAA of 79.5%. Among open-weight agents, Qwen3-32B obtains the highest overall MBNS (0.148) and MAA (69.1%), whereas Phi-4-14B achieves the highest BWR (63.3%). The conventional HPO methods generally trail the stronger agents in overall performance under the same five intervention opportunities. Among these baselines, the BOHB variant obtains the highest overall MBNS (0.018) and MAA (65.3%), while random search achieves the highest BWR (48.9%).

不同任务类别的性能差异很大，并且没有一种方法在每个类别中都表现最佳。 Qwen3-32B 在 NLP 上取得了最高的 MBNS。 Claude Sonnet 4.6 在 CV、TS、Graph 和 LLM 上领先，GPT-5.5 在 RL 上领先，GLM-5.1 在 SL 上领先。这些结果表明，试剂和传统 HPO 方法的相对有效性在很大程度上取决于实验领域。类别级 HPO 结果还表明，尽管整体性能较低，但传统优化在特定领域仍然具有竞争力。Performance varies substantially across task categories, and no method performs best in every category. Qwen3-32B achieves the highest MBNS on NLP. Claude Sonnet 4.6 leads on CV, TS, Graph, and LLM, GPT-5.5 leads on RL, and GLM-5.1 leads on SL. These results demonstrate that the relative effectiveness of agents and conventional HPO methods depends strongly on the experimental domain. The category-level HPO results also show that conventional optimization remains competitive in specific domains despite its lower overall performance.

总体领导力并不意味着单个任务的持续改进。尽管 Claude Sonnet 4.6 获得了最强的综合结果，但其 76.7% 的 BWR 意味着其最终配置在 30 项任务中有 23 项超过了参考基线。 Qwen3-32B 在三个对照种子中获得了 60.0% 的平均 BWR。其余的失败，以及负的和接近零的类别分数，表明即使是强大的代理也可以产生不会改善基线的最终配置。这些发现表明，主要的挑战不仅是确定有用的干预措施，而且是在整个顺序优化过程中完善和保留其好处。Aggregate leadership does not imply consistent improvement across individual tasks. Although Claude Sonnet 4.6 obtains the strongest aggregate results, its BWR of 76.7% means that its final configurations exceed the reference baseline on 23 of the 30 tasks. Qwen3-32B obtains a mean BWR of 60.0% across the three controlled seeds. The remaining failures, together with the negative and near-zero category scores, show that even strong agents can produce final configurations that do not improve the baseline. These findings indicate that the main challenge is not only to identify useful interventions, but also to refine and preserve their benefits throughout the sequential optimization process.

表5Table 5在有限预算协议下取消 Qwen3-32B 的中间实验反馈。Ablation of intermediate experimental feedback for Qwen3-32B under the limited budget protocol.

完整的预算结果。Full Budget Results.为了检查训练预算如何影响顺序优化，我们使用原始存储库的完整训练预算来评估三个选定的代理，如表 3 所示。完整预算增加了所有三个代理的 MBNS 和 MAA。 Qwen3-32B、DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6 的 MBNS 值分别从有限预算协议下的 0.148、0.018 和 0.407 增加到 0.191、0.151 和 0.472。 Claude Sonnet 4.6 也获得了最高的全预算 MAA 89.1%。然而，BWR 的改善并不均匀：Claude Sonnet 4.6 仍保持在 76.7%，而 Qwen3-32B 和 DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B 分别从 60.0% 和 54.4% 下降到 56.7% 和 50.0%。这些变化并不矛盾，因为这三个指标反映了绩效的不同方面。完整的训练可以改变代理的基线性能和最终性能。因此，代理可能会更接近锚点，同时实现更大的平均标准化改进，但在较少的任务上超出相应的完整预算基线。因此，有限预算和全额预算设置提供了有关不同计算约束下优化的补充证据。To examine how the training budget affects sequential optimization, we evaluate three selected agents using the full training budget of the original repositories, as shown in Table 3. The full budget increases both MBNS and MAA for all three agents. The MBNS values of Qwen3-32B, DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6 increase from 0.148, 0.018, and 0.407 under the limited-budget protocol to 0.191, 0.151, and 0.472, respectively. Claude Sonnet 4.6 also achieves the highest full-budget MAA of 89.1%. However, BWR does not improve uniformly: Claude Sonnet 4.6 remains at 76.7%, while Qwen3-32B and DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B decrease from 60.0% and 54.4% to 56.7% and 50.0%, respectively. These changes are not contradictory because the three metrics capture different aspects of performance. Full training can change both the baseline performance and the final performance obtained by an agent. Consequently, an agent may finish closer to the anchor while achieving a larger average normalized improvement, yet exceed the corresponding full-budget baseline on fewer tasks. The limited- and full-budget settings therefore provide complementary evidence about optimization under different computational constraints.

线束消融。Harness Ablation.我们通过将本机 AgentHPOBench 工具与相同任务、干预空间、预算和评分规则下的相应 CLI 工具进行比较，来检查执行工具如何影响性能。结果如表 4 所示。对于 Claude Sonnet 4.6，本机harness 实现了比 Claude Code CLI 更高的 MBNS（0.407 与 0.332），而两种harness 获得相同的 BWR，均为 76.7%。 Claude Code CLI 获得了更高的 MAA（82.2% 对比 79.5%）。同样，对于 GPT-5.5，本机harness 实现了比 Codex CLI 更高的 MBNS（0.305 vs. 0.266），而 Codex CLI 获得了更高的 BWR（70.0% vs. 66.7%）和 MAA（78.7% vs. 76.7%）。这些结果表明，原生工具比两个代理的参考基线产生了更大的相对改进，而 CLI 工具实现了更高的绝对锚点实现。然而，没有一种工具能够在所有指标或任务类别中始终占据主导地位。因此，线束的选择会影响顺序优化过程中产生的配置以及最终的性能。这种敏感性促使使用通用的工具来进行主要比较和单独报告工具效果。We examine how the execution harness affects performance by comparing the native AgentHPOBench harness with corresponding CLI harnesses under the same tasks, intervention spaces, budgets, and scoring rules. The results are shown in Table 4. For Claude Sonnet 4.6, the native harness achieves a higher MBNS than Claude Code CLI (0.407 vs. 0.332), while both harnesses obtain the same BWR of 76.7%. Claude Code CLI achieves a higher MAA (82.2% vs. 79.5%). Similarly, for GPT-5.5, the native harness achieves a higher MBNS than Codex CLI (0.305 vs. 0.266), whereas Codex CLI obtains a higher BWR (70.0% vs. 66.7%) and MAA (78.7% vs. 76.7%). These results show that the native harness produces greater relative improvement over the reference baseline for both agents, while the CLI harnesses achieve higher absolute anchor attainment. However, no harness consistently dominates across all metrics or task categories. Harness choice can therefore affect the configurations produced during sequential optimization and the resulting performance. This sensitivity motivates the use of a common harness for the main comparison and the separate reporting of harness effects.

9

顺序优化轨迹。Sequential Optimization Trajectories.图 3 报告了参考基线和每次后续干预后获得的 MBNS。 Claude Sonnet 4.6 和 Qwen3-32B 在前两次干预期间取得了实质性进展，而 GPT-5.5 在整个轨迹上的进步更加缓慢。其他代理表现出不太稳定的行为。 DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B 在恢复之前最初跌至基线以下，而 Gemma2-2B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 在大部分轨迹中都在基线附近波动。一些代理还表现出非单调细化。例如，Phi-4-14B 在最后一次干预时恢复之前失去了部分早期改善，而 Kimi-2.6 在第四次干预时下降，随后反弹。相比之下，Claude Sonnet 4.6 和 GPT-5.5 在最终干预时达到了最高的 MBNS，这表明后来的反馈仍然可以产生有用的改进。总体而言，当前的智能体可以识别有益的配置，但它们保留和提高早期收益的能力在不同模型中仍然不一致。Figure 3 reports the MBNS obtained after the reference baseline and each subsequent intervention. Claude Sonnet 4.6 and Qwen3-32B achieve substantial gains during the first two interventions, while GPT-5.5 improves more gradually across the trajectory. Other agents exhibit less stable behavior. DeepSeek-R1-Qwen-14B initially falls below the baseline before recovering, whereas Gemma2-2B and Llama-3.1-8B fluctuate around the baseline for most of the trajectory. Several agents also show nonmonotonic refinement. For example, Phi-4-14B loses part of its early improvement before recovering at the final intervention, while Kimi-2.6 declines at the fourth intervention and subsequently rebounds. In contrast, Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.5 achieve their highest MBNS at the final intervention, showing that later feedback can still produce useful refinements. Overall, current agents can identify beneficial configurations, but their ability to preserve and improve earlier gains remains inconsistent across models.

中间反馈消融。Intermediate Feedback Ablation.我们通过在其他相同的协议下评估 Qwen3-32B 来隔离中间实验反馈的贡献。基线观察和所有其他任务输入仍然可用，但每次干预产生的指标和执行日志将从后续决策中保留。如表 5 所示，消除中间反馈可将总体 MBNS 从 0.148 降低至 0.052，将 BWR 从 60.0% 降低至 50.0%，而 MAA 从 69.1% 降低至 65.2%。标准反馈在所有七个任务类别中产生更高的 MBNS。这些结果提供的证据表明，获得中间结果可以改善有限预算协议下 Qwen3-32B 的顺序优化。We isolate the contribution of intermediate experimental feedback by evaluating Qwen3-32B under an otherwise identical protocol. The baseline observation and all other task inputs remain available, but the metrics and execution logs produced by each intervention are withheld from subsequent decisions. As shown in Table 5, removing intermediate feedback reduces overall MBNS from 0.148 to 0.052 and BWR from 60.0% to 50.0%, while MAA decreases from 69.1% to 65.2%. Standard feedback yields higher MBNS in all seven task categories. These results provide evidence that access to intermediate outcomes improves sequential optimization for Qwen3-32B under the limited-budget protocol.

5 结论5 Conclusion

在这项工作中，我们提出了 AgentHPOBench，这是一个评估代理是否可以通过顺序超参数干预来改进可执行机器学习实验的基准。 AgentHPOBench 超越了传统的 HPO 基准测试，要求代理解释来自研究存储库的指标和日志，并将这些证据转换为有效的配置，具有干净的黑盒客观界面。跨 30 个任务的实验表明，当前代理可以改进参考基线，但跨任务域、资源设置和执行工具的性能仍然不均匀，并且通常低于报告的参考性能。这些发现使研究存储库中的 HPO 成为一个具有挑战性的环境，并为开发具有更强的实验诊断和顺序决策能力的代理奠定了基础。In this work, we present AgentHPOBench, a benchmark for evaluating whether agents can improve executable ML experiments through sequential hyperparameter interventions. AgentHPOBench moves beyond conventional HPO benchmarks with clean black box objective interfaces by requiring agents to interpret metrics and logs from research repositories and convert this evidence into valid configurations. Experiments across 30 tasks show that current agents can improve reference baselines, but performance remains uneven across task domains, resource settings, and execution harnesses, and often remains below the reported reference performance. These findings establish HPO in research repositories as a challenging setting and provide a foundation for developing agents with stronger capabilities for experimental diagnosis and sequential decision making.

表6Table 6AgentHPOBench 任务套件。每项任务都是由一个可执行存储库和一个可评分目标指标构建的。AgentHPOBench task suite. Each task is built from one executable repository and one scoreable target metric.

6 附录6 Appendix

本补充材料提供了有关实验方案、任务套件、参考基线和实施设置的更多详细信息。它还提供了完整的结果、补充分析和定性示例，以补充主论文中的发现。This supplementary material provides additional details on the experimental protocol, task suite, reference baselines, and implementation settings. It also presents complete results, supplementary analyses, and qualitative examples that complement the findings in the main paper.

7 AgentHPOBench 任务套件7 AgentHPOBench Task Suite

AgentHPOBench 包含 30 个任务，这些任务取自 30 个不同的可执行 ML 存储库，这些存储库与可用的最新论文相关 [37-61]。表 6 列出了完整的任务套件。锚点取自论文、官方存储库报告或记录的完整设置复制品。AgentHPOBench contains 30 tasks drawn from 30 distinct executable ML repositories associated with recent papers when available [37–61]. Table 6 lists the full task suite. The anchor is taken from a paper, an official repository report, or a documented full setting reproduction.

表 7 报告了在有限预算和全额预算设置下用于评分的参考基线性能。每个任务的基线配置在设置中都是固定的。完整预算协议在适用的情况下扩大了特定任务的培训预算。对于预训练推理任务和没有明显可扩展训练阶段的任务，评估协议保持不变，因此预计基线值相同。例如，ART 2048 在两种设置中都对 25 场比赛使用相同的固定评估，每场比赛最多 70 步，并且其基线启发式没有赢得任何一场比赛，获胜率为零。对于全预算研究，每个选定的代理执行固定基线配置，并将三个观察值的中值用作该任务的通用全预算参考。值以表 6 中定义的公制单位报告，并四舍五入到小数点后三位。所有分数都是根据相应的全精度值计算的。Table 7 reports the reference baseline performance used for scoring under the limited- and full-budget settings. The baseline configuration of each task is fixed across settings. The full-budget protocol expands the task-specific training budget where applicable. For pretrained inference tasks and tasks without a distinct scalable training stage, the evaluation protocol remains unchanged, so identical baseline values are expected. For example, ART 2048 uses the same fixed evaluation of 25 games with at most 70 moves per game in both settings, and its baseline heuristic wins none of these games, yielding a win rate of zero. For the full-budget study, each selected agent executes the fixed baseline configuration, and the median of the three observations is used as the common full-budget reference for that task. Values are reported in the metric units defined in Table 6 and rounded to three decimal places. All scores are computed from the corresponding full-precision values.

8 代理提示模板8 Agent Prompt Template

AgentHPOBench 通过结构化决策模式标准化代理接口。在每个干预步骤中，任务脚本都会构建一个特定于任务的上下文块，其中包含任务描述、目标指标、指标方向、当前配置、允许的搜索空间、论文或存储库锚点、基线结果以及先前可用的试验历史记录。然后将此上下文传递到决策后端，并返回AgentHPOBench standardizes the agent interface through a structured decision schema. At each intervention step, the task script constructs a task-specific context block that contains the task description, target metric, metric direction, current configuration, allowed search space, paper or repository anchor, baseline result, and previous trial history when available. This context is then passed to the decision backend, and the returned

干预在执行之前被解析为结构化配置。图 4 显示了用于本地开放权重代理的共享提示模板，而图 5 显示了用于基于 API 的代理的紧凑决策提示。图 6 提供了一个特定于任务的上下文示例，说明了在请求下一次干预之前如何提供具体的试验证据和允许的搜索空间。我们使用“搜索空间”来表示特定于任务的允许干预空间，即向代理公开的可配置字段以及每个字段的有效值，而不是代理的解码超参数。intervention is parsed into a structured configuration before execution. Figure 4 shows the shared prompt template used for local open-weight agents, while Figure 5 shows the compact decision prompt used for API-based agents. Figure 6 provides an example task-specific context, illustrating how concrete trial evidence and the allowed search space are supplied before requesting the next intervention. We use “search space” to denote the task-specific allowed intervention space, namely the configurable fields exposed to the agent and the valid values for each field, rather than the decoding hyperparameters of the agent.

本地和 API 模板保留各自代理实现所使用的接口，而不是强制执行相同的表面措辞。因此，提示构造、响应解析、配置验证、重试和其他线束行为是评估的代理系统的一部分。由此产生的比较应解释为完整代理管道之间的比较，而不是底层语言模型的孤立排名。The local and API templates preserve the interfaces used by their respective agent implementations rather than enforcing identical surface wording. Prompt construction, response parsing, configuration validation, retries, and other harness behavior are therefore part of the evaluated agent system. The resulting comparisons should be interpreted as comparisons between complete agent pipelines, not as isolated rankings of the underlying language models.

9 实施和再现性细节9 Implementation and Reproducibility Details

通用执行协议。Common execution protocol.除非另有说明，所有结果均使用有限预算协议。每个任务首先执行固定的参考基线，然后允许五次连续干预。基线和每次干预都使用相同的特定于任务的数据分割、指标、干预空间和执行预算。有限的设置大约使用相应原始培训或评估预算的 10%。全预算研究仅更改此执行预算，并保留任务定义、提示、干预空间、五决策协议、结果模式和评分管道。每个决策必须以结构化 NEW_CONFIG 格式返回一个完整的配置。省略的字段保留其当前值，并且在执行之前对配置进行验证并将其限制到特定于任务的离散干预空间。报告的任务结果是第五次干预后的指标，而不是最佳中间指标。Unless stated otherwise, all results use the limited-budget protocol. Each task first executes a fixed reference baseline and then permits five sequential interventions. The baseline and every intervention use the same task-specific data split, metric, intervention space, and execution budget. The limited setting uses approximately 10% of the corresponding original training or evaluation budget. The full-budget study changes only this execution budget and retains the task definition, prompt, intervention space, five-decision protocol, result schema, and scoring pipeline. Each decision must return one complete configuration in the structured NEW_CONFIG format. Omitted fields retain their current values, and configurations are validated and clamped to the task-specific discrete intervention space before execution. The reported task result is the metric after intervention five, rather than the best intermediate metric.

该基准测试优化了每个上游存储库公开的目标。一些存储库报告测试集指标，或由验证性能选择的检查点的测试指标，并且此存储库定义的目标在顺序决策过程中是可见的。 AgentHPOBench 因此评估优化The benchmark optimizes the objective exposed by each upstream repository. Some repositories report a testset metric, or a test metric at the checkpoint selected by validation performance, and this repository-defined objective is visible during sequential decision making. AgentHPOBench therefore evaluates optimization

17

本地开重代理提示Local open-weight agent prompt

1 您是一位机器学习专家研究员，帮助优化模型训练的超参数。 2 3 ## 任务 4 在剩余预算内优化超参数以达到 {optimization_goal} 目标指标。 5 - 指标方向：{metric_direction} 6 7 ## 当前训练进度 8 - 当前周期：{epoch} 9 - 剩余预算：{budget_remaining} 周期，共 {total_budget} 10 - 决策编号：{decision_number} 11 12 ## 最近的训练日志 13 {training_log} 14 15 ## 当前超参数 16 {current_config} 17 18 ## 超参数搜索空间 19 {search_space} 20 21 ## 说明 22 - 保持保守，除非运行明显稳定或不稳定。 23 - 仅返回搜索空间内的值。 24 - 如果训练正常改善，则保留当前配置是可以接受的。 25 - 仅输出纯文本。 26 - 不要使用 Markdown 代码围栏、XML 标签或额外的部分。 27 - 按照下面的确切标题进行操作。 28 29 ## 响应格式 30 推理：1 You are an expert machine learning researcher helping optimize hyperparameters for model training. 2 3 ## Task 4 Optimize hyperparameters to {optimization_goal} the target metric within the remaining budget . 5 - Metric Direction: {metric_direction} 6 7 ## Current Training Progress 8 - Current Epoch: {epoch} 9 - Budget Remaining: {budget_remaining} epochs out of {total_budget} total 10 - Decision Number: {decision_number} 11 12 ## Recent Training Logs 13 {training_log} 14 15 ## Current Hyperparameters 16 {current_config} 17 18 ## Hyperparameter Search Space 19 {search_space} 20 21 ## Instructions 22 - Be conservative unless the run is clearly plateauing or unstable. 23 - Only return values inside the search space. 24 - If training is improving normally, keeping the current config is acceptable. 25 - Output plain text only. 26 - Do not use markdown code fences, XML tags, or extra sections. 27 - Follow the exact headers below. 28 29 ## Response Format 30 REASONING:31 NEW_CONFIG：{“learning_rate”：0.05，“batch_size”：128，“optimizer”：“sgd”} 32 置信度：0.831 NEW_CONFIG: {"learning_rate": 0.05, "batch_size": 128, "optimizer": "sgd"} 32 CONFIDENCE: 0.8

图6Figure 6用于请求下一次干预的特定任务上下文示例。Example task-specific context used to request the next intervention.

开放权重决策模型。Open-weight decision models.表 8 列出了有限预算协议下六个开放权重代理的确切检查点和实现的生成代币使用情况。我们使用检查点的聊天模板和 bfloat16 权重通过 Hugging Face Transformers 加载所有检查点。代理解码是确定性的：温度为 0、采样被禁用、可选思维输出被禁用，以便生成从请求的结构化决策开始。令牌计数是使用与每个检查点关联的令牌生成器根据生成的决策文本计算的。它们不包括输入提示标记。每个检查点都会贡献来自 30 项任务、五项干预措施和三个实验种子的 450 个决策。Table 8 lists the exact checkpoints and realized generated-token usage for the six open-weight agents under the limited-budget protocol. We load all checkpoints with Hugging Face Transformers using the checkpoint’s chat template and bfloat16 weights. Agent decoding is deterministic: temperature is 0, sampling is disabled, and optional thinking output is disabled so that generation begins with the requested structured decision. Token counts are computed from the generated decision text using the tokenizer associated with each checkpoint. They exclude input-prompt tokens. Each checkpoint contributes 450 decisions from 30 tasks, five interventions, and three experiment seeds.

基于 API 的代理。API-based agents.我们使用与开放权重代理相同的五个逻辑决策、任务上下文、输出模式和配置验证来评估 DeepSeek-V4-Pro、GPT-5.5、GLM-4.7、GLM-5.1、Kimi-2.6 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6。当端点公开此控件时，请求温度设置为 0。对于每个决策，结果跟踪都会记录该工具返回的提供者和模型标识符、原始响应、时间戳、令牌使用字段（如果可用）以及重试或错误元数据。每个 API 代理都通过一次完整的、带时间戳的、经过审核的 30 任务运行进行评估。此选择不作为受控种子复制呈现：托管端点不公开不可变的检查点构建、服务副本、批处理状态或可再现的端到端随机种子，并且这些服务端状态可能独立于基准而变化。因此，重复 API 请求将测量模型和服务更改的混合，而不是由受控本地运行隔离的基准随机性。表 9 报告了提供商记录的 API 评估活动的使用情况。We evaluate DeepSeek-V4-Pro, GPT-5.5, GLM-4.7, GLM-5.1, Kimi-2.6, and Claude Sonnet 4.6 using the same five logical decisions, task contexts, output schema, and configuration validation as the open-weight agents. The request temperature is set to 0 when the endpoint exposes this control. For every decision, the result trace records the provider and model identifier returned by the harness, the raw response, timestamp, token-usage fields when available, and retry or error metadata. Each API agent is evaluated with one complete, timestamped, audited 30-task run. This choice is not presented as a controlled seed replicate: hosted endpoints do not expose an immutable checkpoint build, serving replica, batching state, or a reproducible end-to-end random seed, and these service-side states may change independently of the benchmark. Repeating an API request would therefore measure a mixture of model and serving changes rather than the benchmark stochasticity isolated by the controlled local runs. Table 9 reports the provider-recorded usage of the API evaluation campaign.

每个 API 代理在 30 项任务中做出 150 个已接受的逻辑决策，而活动记录了 1,231-1,695 个请求，或者每个已接受的决策大约有 8.2-11.3 个请求。请求总数包括重试、解析或验证恢复以及其他线束级别调用，因此不是额外的干预机会。提供者记录的令牌计数包括提示令牌和生成令牌，并且不能与表 8 中的仅生成输出计数直接比较。Each API agent makes 150 accepted logical decisions across the 30 tasks, while the campaign records 1,231– 1,695 requests, or approximately 8.2–11.3 requests per accepted decision. The request totals include retries, parsing or validation recovery, and other harness-level calls and therefore are not additional intervention opportunities. Provider-recorded token counts include both prompt and generated tokens and are not directly comparable with the generated-output-only counts in Table 8.

种子、重复和聚合。Seeds, repetitions, and aggregation.我们使用实验种子运行每个开放重量代理和每个传统 HPO 基线We run every open-weight agent and each conventional HPO baseline with experiment seeds𝑠ε{0∈{0, 1 ,42}。相同的三个种子在基准测试工具、优化器和随机任务执行中一致使用，而由上游协议固定的数据集分割保持不变。随机搜索、TPE 和固定预算 BOHB 式方法使用与代理相同的参考基线、离散干预空间和五种配置评估。他们的提案规则详见附录 11.1。对于每种受控方法，每个任务和种子的有界归一化分数、基线获胜指标和锚点实现都是独立计算的。然后计算每个种子的类别和总体指标，并报告为三个种子的算术平均值。表 10 还报告了相应的样本标准差。这为每个基准任务保留了相同的权重。本地代理检查点、解码设置、运行时环境和任务随机状态均受到显式控制，因此这些重复量化了对基准执行随机性的敏感性，而不是对提供者端变化的敏感性。42}. The same three seeds are used consistently across the benchmark harness, optimizer, and stochastic task execution, while dataset splits fixed by an upstream protocol remain unchanged. Random search, TPE, and the fixed-budget BOHB-style method use the same reference baseline, discrete intervention space, and five configuration evaluations as the agents. Their proposal rules are detailed in Appendix 11.1. For each controlled method, the bounded normalized score, baseline win indicator, and anchor attainment are computed independently for every task and seed. Category and overall metrics are then computed for each seed and reported as the arithmetic mean across the three seeds. Table 10 additionally reports the corresponding sample standard deviation. This preserves equal weight for every benchmark task. The local agent checkpoints, decoding settings, runtime environment, and task random states are explicitly controlled, so these repetitions quantify sensitivity to benchmark execution stochasticity rather than provider-side variation.

无中间反馈消融。No intermediate feedback ablation.无中间反馈条件与在任务和复制种子级别运行的标准反馈配对。它使用相应标准反馈运行记录的准确基线配置、目标指标和基线观察，而不是独立地重新评估基线。因此，基线指标可供代理使用。然而，对于所有五个决策，可见历史仅包含此固定基线观察。干预措施一到四产生的指标、辅助输出和日志将被保留。拟议的干预措施仍正常执行和记录。因此，消融隔离了对中间实验证据的访问，同时保持初始观察、评估种子、任务预算、搜索空间和决策模型固定。The no intermediate feedback condition is paired with the standard feedback run at the task and replicate-seed level. It uses the exact baseline configuration, target metric, and baseline observation recorded by the corresponding standard-feedback run rather than independently re-evaluating the baseline. The baseline metric is therefore available to the agent. However, for all five decisions, the visible history contains only this fixed baseline observation. Metrics, auxiliary outputs, and logs produced by interventions one through four are withheld. The proposed interventions are still executed and recorded normally. Thus, the ablation isolates access to intermediate experimental evidence while holding the initial observation, evaluator seed, task budget, search space, and decision model fixed.

20

执行环境和再现性资源。Execution environment and reproducibility resources.受控实验在配备 143 个 NVIDIA H200 GPU 的 Linux 开发机器上运行The controlled experiments run on Linux development machines equipped with NVIDIA H200 GPUs with 143,771 MiB 可见内存。编排层使用Python 3.10或更高版本。由于 30 个任务依赖于异构上游存储库，因此每个任务适配器都会调用其存储库特定的 Conda 环境，而不是强制所有任务进入一个依赖堆栈。模型权重和数据集在执行前下载，报告的运行使用离线 Hugging Face 模式。771 MiB of visible memory. The orchestration layer uses Python 3.10 or newer. Because the 30 tasks depend on heterogeneous upstream repositories, each task adapter invokes its repository-specific Conda environment rather than forcing all tasks into one dependency stack. Model weights and datasets are downloaded before execution, and the reported runs use offline Hugging Face modes.

为了可重复性，我们记录了每个任务的上游存储库版本、基准调整、环境和资产准备要求、模型检查点、任务种子、基线配置、指标提取规则、干预空间、执行预算和评分参考。公共 GitHub 存储库中将提供详细的设置说明和机器可读的任务规范。For reproducibility, we record the upstream repository version, benchmark adaptations, environment and asset-preparation requirements, model checkpoint, task seeds, baseline configuration, metric extraction rule, intervention space, execution budget, and scoring reference for every task. Detailed setup instructions and machine-readable task specifications will be provided in the public GitHub repository.

结果验证。Result Validation.我们仅包含完成参考基线和所有五种干预措施的任务运行，并在每个步骤中记录有效的配置和指标。中断或格式错误的运行将被排除并在任务级别重新运行。所有报告的聚合都是根据完整的任务记录计算得出的。公共 GitHub 存储库包括相应的验证和聚合实用程序。We include only task runs that complete the reference baseline and all five interventions, with a valid configuration and metric recorded at every step. Interrupted or malformed runs are excluded and rerun at the task level. All reported aggregates are computed from complete task records. The public GitHub repository includes the corresponding validation and aggregation utilities.

10 评分和汇总详细信息10 Scoring and Aggregation Details

对于固定预算设置For a fixed budget setting𝑟, 让, let𝑦𝑡,(𝑟 0)表示任务的参考基线性能)denote the reference baseline performance for task𝑡_报告于表7中，𝑡_reported in Table 7,让_𝑦𝑡，𝑘let _𝑦𝑡,𝑘(𝑟)表示干预后的结果)denote the result after intervention𝑘，并让, and let𝑎𝑡_表示论文或存储库锚点。主要任务𝑎𝑡_denote the paper or repository anchor.The main task结果是_𝑦𝑡,𝐾result is _𝑦𝑡,𝐾(𝑟），这是最终干预后得到的，而不是选择最好的中间结果。), which is obtained after the final intervention rather than selected as the best intermediate result.该约定衡量优化器在观察完整轨迹后是否保留或改进其改进。This convention measures whether an optimizer preserves or refines its improvements after observing the complete trajectory.

在评分之前，每个基线、干预结果和锚点都会转换为其任务中的通用数字单位。例如，当相应的锚点报告为百分比时，表示为分数的准确度将转换为百分比。让Before scoring, every baseline, intervention result, and anchor is converted to a common numeric unit within its task. For example, an accuracy represented as a fraction is converted to a percentage when the corresponding anchor is reported as a percentage. Let𝑔𝑡 ( 𝑧）=) =𝑧对于一个值越高越好的指标for a metric in which higher values are better and𝑔𝑡 ( 𝑧 ) = − 𝑧对于一个值越低越好的指标。我们将标准化分数定义为for a metric in which lower values are better. We define the normalized score as

值为 0 与参考基线匹配，值为 1 与锚点匹配，负值表示退化。由于锚定间隙的基线较小可能会给一项任务带来不成比例的影响，因此我们在聚合之前绑定每个任务分数：A value of 0 matches the reference baseline, a value of 1 matches the anchor, and a negative value indicates degradation. Because a small baseline to anchor gap can give one task disproportionate influence, we bound each task score before aggregation:

平均锚定达到度为The mean anchor attainment is

因此，MBNS 衡量共同参考基线的有限改进，BWR 衡量积极改进的覆盖范围，MAA 衡量报告参考性能的绝对实现情况。当结果超出其锚点时，MAA 可能会超过 100%。Thus, MBNS measures bounded improvement over the common reference baseline, BWR measures the coverage of positive improvements, and MAA measures absolute attainment of reported reference performance. MAA may exceed 100% when a result surpasses its anchor.

11 附加实验方案和结果11 Additional Experimental Protocols and Results

本节提供了传统 HPO 基线和主论文中报告的中间反馈消融的实施细节和支持结果。所有方法都使用由基准任务规范定义的特定任务干预空间，从相同的有限预算参考基线开始，接收五个干预机会，并使用第五次干预后的结果进行评分。表 6 总结了相应的任务、目标、度量方向和锚点。This section provides implementation details and supporting results for the conventional HPO baselines and the intermediate feedback ablation reported in the main paper. All methods use the task-specific intervention spaces defined by the benchmark task specifications, begin from the same limited-budget reference baseline, receive five intervention opportunities, and are scored using the result after the fifth intervention. Table 6 summarizes the corresponding tasks, objectives, metric directions, and anchors.

11.1 传统的 HPO 基线11.1 Conventional HPO Baselines

实施。Implementation.随机搜索根据其允许的离散值独立且统一地对每个可配置字段进行采样。我们使用种子 0、1 和 42 来评估随机搜索。Random search samples every configurable field independently and uniformly from its allowed discrete values. We evaluate random search with seeds 0, 1, and 42.

TPE 基线使用每次干预之前可用的观察结果，按照目标指标的方向对先前的配置进行排名。对提案进行统一抽样，直到至少有两个已执行的观察结果可用。然后 TPE 分配最好的 ⌈0The TPE baseline uses the observations available before each intervention to rank previous configurations in the direction of the target metric. Proposals are sampled uniformly until at least two executed observations are available. TPE then assigns the best ⌈0. 35 𝑛⌉ 的⌉ of the𝑛对好的集合进行观察，并根据平滑的好坏频率比对每个字段进行采样。加法平滑常数为 1observations to the good set and samples each field according to its smoothed good to bad frequency ratio. The additive smoothing constant is 1.0，并且提案使用概率为 0 的均匀探索0, and a proposal uses uniform exploration with probability 0.每个字段 15 个。15 for each field.

由于有限预算协议中的每次干预都接收相同的训练预算，因此标准多保真资源分配不可用。因此，我们对 BOHB 实施了固定预算调整。对提案进行统一抽样，直到至少有两个已执行的观察结果可用。后来的提案对观察结果进行排名，保留最好的 ⌈Because every intervention in the limited budget protocol receives the same training budget, standard multi fidelity resource allocation is not available. We therefore implement a fixed budget adaptation of BOHB. Proposals are sampled uniformly until at least two executed observations are available. Later proposals rank the observations, retain the best ⌈𝑛/3⌉ 作为当前的精英集，选择具有基于排名权重的父代，并以概率 0 变异每个字段/3⌉ as the current elite set, select a parent with rank based weights, and mutate each field with probability 0.35. 从平滑的好坏频率比中以概率 0 采样变异场35. A mutated field is sampled from the smoothed good to bad frequency ratio with probability 0.5，否则统一。随机搜索、TPE 和 BOHB 改编均使用种子 {0 进行评估5 and uniformly otherwise. Random search, TPE, and this BOHB adaptation are each evaluated with seeds {0, 1 ,42}。因此，BOHB结果评估其在常见五种干预协议下的配置选择策略，而不是标准BOHB的资源分配组件。42}. Thus, the BOHB result evaluates its configuration selection policy under the common five intervention protocol, rather than the resource allocation component of standard BOHB.

传统的优化器对配置和标量目标值进行操作，而代理还通过其本机决策接口处理任务描述和实验反馈。因此，这些基线比较了共同执行预算下的完整优化系统。所有方法均在相同的五项干预执行预算下进行评估。由于每个试验都会收到相同的资源预算，因此 BOHB 样式基线会在此设置中评估其配置建议组件。多保真资源调度超出了协议的范围。The conventional optimizers operate on configurations and scalar target values, while the agents additionally process the task description and experimental feedback through their native decision interface. Accordingly, these baselines compare complete optimization systems under a common execution budget. All methods are evaluated under the same five-intervention execution budget. Because every trial receives the same resource budget, the BOHB-style baseline evaluates its configuration-proposal component in this setting. Multi-fidelity resource scheduling is outside the scope of the protocol.

11.2 统计稳健性11.2 Statistical Robustness

我们还使用 20,000 个类别分层配对引导重采样来评估对基准任务套件组成的敏感性。每次重新采样都会保留每个研究类别中的任务数量，并且所有方法都使用相同的采样任务。对于开放权重智能体和传统的 HPO 基线，首先对三个受控种子对任务级 BNS 和锚点实现值进行平均，然后重新采样。因此，所得间隔量化了与任务构成相关的不确定性，而表 10 中的标准差量化了受控执行之间的变化。对于评估一次的 API 代理，这些间隔仅反映对基准任务组合的敏感性，不应被解释为重复 API 执行的不确定性。We additionally assess sensitivity to the composition of the benchmark task suite using 20,000 categorystratified paired bootstrap resamples. Each resample preserves the number of tasks in every research category, and the same sampled tasks are used for all methods. For open-weight agents and conventional HPO baselines, the task-level BNS and anchor attainment values are first averaged over the three controlled seeds and then resampled. The resulting intervals therefore quantify uncertainty associated with task composition, whereas the standard deviations in Table 10 quantify variation across controlled executions. For API agents, which are evaluated once, these intervals reflect sensitivity to benchmark task composition only and should not be interpreted as uncertainty across repeated API executions.

区间定义。Interval definitions.任务引导 95% 置信区间是通过在每个研究类别中重复对任务进行替换重采样、重新计算每次重采样的聚合指标并取结果分布的第 2.5 个和第 97.5 个百分位来获得的。它衡量聚合结果对基准任务套件组成的敏感性。配对 MBNS 差异计算为 MBNS(A task-bootstrap 95% confidence interval is obtained by repeatedly resampling tasks with replacement within each research category, recomputing the aggregate metric for each resample, and taking the 2.5th and 97.5th percentiles of the resulting distribution. It measures the sensitivity of an aggregate result to the composition of the benchmark task suite. A paired MBNS difference is computed as MBNS(𝐴 ) − MBNS( 𝐵）对两种方法使用相同的重采样任务。正差异有利于方法 A，而负差异有利于方法 B。如果其 95% 置信区间包含零，则观察到的排序在任务构成变化的情况下不稳定。) using the same resampled tasks for both methods. A positive difference favors Method A, while a negative difference favors Method B. If its 95% confidence interval includes zero, the observed ordering is not stable under variation in task composition.

引导区间表明，基准构成带来了不可忽略的不确定性，特别是对于收益集中在少数类别或任务的方法而言。 Claude Sonnet 4.6 保留了四个报告摘要中最强的点估计。 Qwen3-32B 和 Phi-4-14B 在开放权重智能体中具有类似的任务构成不确定性。在最终步骤标准下，三个传统的 HPO 区间也很宽泛。这些区间旨在作为稳健性诊断，而不是多重比较显着性检验。因此，方法之间的微小差异应与下面报告的配对比较一起解释。The bootstrap intervals show that benchmark composition contributes non-negligible uncertainty, particularly for methods whose gains are concentrated in a small number of categories or tasks. Claude Sonnet 4.6 retains the strongest point estimates across the four reported summaries. Qwen3-32B and Phi-4-14B have similar task-composition uncertainty among open-weight agents. The three conventional HPO intervals are also broad under the final-step criterion. These intervals are intended as a robustness diagnostic rather than a multiple-comparison significance test. Small differences between methods should therefore be interpreted together with the paired comparisons reported below.

11.3 迄今为止最佳性能11.3 Best-So-Far Performance

主要结果使用第五次干预时产生的配置，该配置评估方法是否在整个轨迹中保留和完善改进。作为补充诊断，表 12 报告了干预措施一到五中观察到的最佳结果。对于每项任务，根据特定于任务的度量方向的最佳干预是根据主要结果中使用的相同公共参考基线进行评分的。参考基线本身不包括在候选干预措施中。对于使用三个种子评估的方法，在对种子和任务进行平均之前，对每个任务和种子独立执行评分，以匹配主要结果中使用的聚合。The main results use the configuration produced at the fifth intervention, which evaluates whether a method preserves and refines improvements throughout the full trajectory. As a complementary diagnostic, Table 12 reports the best result observed among interventions one through five. For each task, the best intervention according to the task-specific metric direction is scored against the same common reference baseline used in the main results. The reference baseline itself is not included among the candidate interventions. For methods evaluated with three seeds, scoring is performed independently for each task and seed before averaging across seeds and tasks, matching the aggregation used in the main results.

表11Table 11有限预算协议下评估方法的任务构成稳健性。间隔是 30 项任务的 20,000 个类别分层配对引导重采样的百分位数 95% 置信区间。中值 BNS 和 BWR 是点估计值。Task-composition robustness of the evaluated methods under the limited-budget protocol. Intervals are percentile 95% confidence intervals from 20,000 category-stratified paired bootstrap resamples of the 30 tasks. Median BNS and BWR are point estimates.

该分析将干预预算中发现的最佳配置的质量与在最后一步保留它的能力区分开来。 Claude Sonnet 4.6 仍然是整体最强的方法，MBNS 为 0.469，BWR 为 90.0%，MAA 为 80.5%。它还在 CV、Graph、LLM 和 SL 上领先，而 GPT-5.5 在 RL 上领先，在 TS 上领先于 TPE，在 NLP 上领先于随机搜索。与他们的最后一步结果相比，在迄今为止最佳选择下，三个传统 HPO 基线的总体 MBNS 提高了 0.273-0.411，这表明他们经常发现有用的配置，但并不能始终如一地完成它们。This analysis distinguishes the quality of the best configuration discovered within the intervention budget from the ability to retain it at the final step. Claude Sonnet 4.6 remains the strongest method overall, reaching an MBNS of 0.469, a BWR of 90.0%, and an MAA of 80.5%. It also leads on CV, Graph, LLM, and SL, while GPT-5.5 leads on RL, TPE on TS, and random search on NLP. Compared with their final-step results, the overall MBNS of the three conventional HPO baselines improves by 0.273–0.411 under best-so-far selection, indicating that they often discover useful configurations but do not consistently finish with them.

在这种现有风格的观点下，随机搜索、TPE 和 BOHB 变体分别获得 0.325、0.298 和 0.291 的总体 MBNS 值，超过了所有评估的开放权重代理。 Phi-4-14B和Qwen3-32B达到0.197和0.195。这种排名逆转表明，最后一步指标将搜索质量与保留或完善早期收益的能力结合起来，而标准 HPO 通常会返回现有的。最强的 API 代理在迄今为止最佳选择下仍然具有竞争力，Claude Sonnet 4.6 和 GPT-5.5 分别达到 0.469 和 0.347。下面报告了相应的种子变化和任务引导间隔。Under this incumbent-style view, random search, TPE, and the BOHB variant obtain overall MBNS values of 0.325, 0.298, and 0.291, respectively, exceeding all evaluated open-weight agents. Phi-4-14B and Qwen3-32B reach 0.197 and 0.195. This ranking reversal clarifies that the final-step metric combines search quality with the ability to preserve or refine an earlier gain, whereas standard HPO commonly returns the incumbent. The strongest API agents remain competitive under best-so-far selection, with Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.5 reaching 0.469 and 0.347. The corresponding seed variation and task-bootstrap intervals are reported below.

表 13 将执行变化与基准组成的敏感性分开。三种传统的 HPO 方法具有重叠的迄今为止最佳任务引导间隔，Phi-4-14B 和 Qwen3-32B 也是如此。Table 13 separates execution variation from sensitivity to benchmark composition. The three conventional HPO methods have overlapping best-so-far task-bootstrap intervals, as do Phi-4-14B and Qwen3-32B.

表 14 直接引导了所选方法之间的任务级别差异。在最后一步标准下，Claude Sonnet 4.6 相对于 GPT-5.5 具有正的配对差异，Qwen3-32B 相对于 BOHB 变体具有正的差异。 Phi-4-14B 与 Qwen3-32B 的间隔包括零。在迄今为止最佳选择下，Claude Sonnet 4.6 仍然领先于 GPT-5.5，而 GPT-5.5 与随机搜索的间隔包括零。随机搜索相对于 Phi-4-14B 具有正配对差异，而其与 TPE 和 BOHB 变体的差异尚无定论。这些结果支持报告两种选择标准，而不将小的点估计差异视为最终排名。Table 14 directly bootstraps task-level differences between selected methods. Under the final-step criterion, Claude Sonnet 4.6 has a positive paired difference relative to GPT-5.5, and Qwen3-32B has a positive difference relative to the BOHB variant. The interval for Phi-4-14B versus Qwen3-32B includes zero. Under best-so-far selection, Claude Sonnet 4.6 remains ahead of GPT-5.5, while the interval for GPT-5.5 versus random search includes zero. Random search has a positive paired difference relative to Phi-4-14B, whereas its differences from TPE and the BOHB variant are inconclusive. These results support reporting both selection criteria without treating small point-estimate differences as definitive rankings.

Holding the task, baseline, and intervention space fixed reveals distinct responses to the same type of experimental feedback. Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.5 recover from harmful initial changes and preserve subsequent improvements. Qwen3-32B also recovers but stops exploring after a modest gain, whereas Llama-3.1-8B discards its strongest configuration at the final intervention. Gemma2-2B repeatedly executes the baseline without testing an alternative.

12 公制灵敏度12 Metric Sensitivity

公制灵敏度。Metric sensitivity.有些任务在有限预算参考基线和报告的锚点之间存在很小的差距。在这种情况下，适度的执行噪声可能会产生相对较大的标准化变化。将每个任务分数限制为 [−1Some tasks have a small gap between the limited-budget reference baseline and the reported anchor. In such cases, modest execution noise can produce a relatively large normalized change. Bounding each task score to [−1,1] 防止一项任务产生任意大的贡献，但不会消除小分母附近的敏感性，并可能导致分数饱和。因此，我们与 MBNS 一起报告 BWR、MAA、中位 BNS、任务级结果和任务组合引导间隔。当前的基准测试不会单独估计重复无操作的基于噪声的最小有意义差异1] prevents an arbitrarily large contribution from one task, but does not remove sensitivity near a small denominator and can cause score saturation. We therefore report BWR, MAA, median BNS, task-level results, and task-composition bootstrap intervals alongside MBNS. The current benchmark does not separately estimate a noise-based minimum meaningful difference from repeated no-op

13 详细实验结果13 Detailed Experimental Results

本节报告主要有限预算评估的每个任务的完整结果。表 7 中提供了相应的参考基线值。表 16-20 报告了五次连续干预措施中每次干预后观察到的原始指标。对于开放权重代理，原始指标是种子 {0This section reports the complete per-task results for the main limited budget evaluation. The corresponding reference baseline values are provided in Table 7. Tables 16–20 report the raw metric observed after each of the five sequential interventions. For open-weight agents, raw metrics are averaged over seeds {0, 1 ,42}在每个任务和干预中，同时计算每个种子的有界标准化分数和锚点达到值，然后求平均值。 API 代理条目与其单个审核评估相对应。值以与表 6 中相应任务锚点相同的单位和方向显示。原始值四舍五入到小数点后三位，而所有派生分数都是根据记录轨迹中的完整精度值计算的。这些表格显示了每个代理在干预措施中是否有所改善、停滞或下降，而不是仅报告最终总分。42} at each task and intervention, while bounded normalized scores and anchor attainment values are computed per seed and then averaged. API-agent entries correspond to their single audited evaluation. Values are shown in the same unit and direction as the corresponding task anchor in Table 6. Raw values are rounded to three decimal places, whereas all derived scores are computed from the full precision values in the recorded traces. These tables show whether each agent improves, plateaus, or degrades across interventions instead of only reporting a final aggregate score.

第五次干预提供用于总分的最终结果。表 21-25 使用每项任务的共同有限预算基线将干预结果转换为有界标准化分数。表 26-30 报告了相应的锚定达到值。相邻干预中重复的原始值可以表明代理在执行后保留相同的配置或获得相同的舍入度量。非单调轨迹是预期的，因为每次干预都被评估为一个新的存储库实验，而不是作为先前试验中保留的最佳结果。The fifth intervention provides the final result used for aggregate scoring. Tables 21–25 convert the intervention results into bounded normalized scores using the common limited budget baseline for each task. Tables 26–30 report the corresponding anchor attainment values. Repeated raw values across adjacent interventions can indicate that an agent retained the same configuration or obtained the same rounded metric after execution. Nonmonotonic trajectories are expected because each intervention is evaluated as a new repository experiment rather than as a best result retained across previous trials.

14 完整的预算和利用消融细节14 Full Budget and Harness Ablation Details

完整预算评估使用与有限预算评估相同的任务定义、代理界面、结果模式、评分规则和五种干预协议。相反，每个基线和干预措施都是使用原始存储库的完整培训或评估预算来执行的。完整干预水平的原始结果如表 31-35 所示。有界归一化分数如表 36-40 所示，锚定达到值如表 41-45 所示。原始指标显示为小数点后三位，而所有派生分数均使用相应的完整精度值。The full budget evaluation uses the same task definitions, agent interface, result schema, scoring rules, and five intervention protocol as the limited budget evaluation. Each baseline and intervention is instead executed with the full training or evaluation budget of the original repository. The complete intervention level raw results are shown in Tables 31–35. The bounded normalized scores are shown in Tables 36–40, and the anchor attainment values are shown in Tables 41–45. Raw metrics are displayed to three decimal places, while all derived scores use the corresponding full precision values.

线束消融使 AgentHPOBench 任务套件、干预预算、结果模式和评分管道保持固定，同时更改骨干代理使用的外部执行线束。对于 Claude Sonnet 4.6，比较是在本机 AgentHPOBench 工具和 Claude Code CLI 之间进行的。对于 GPT-5.5，比较是在本机 AgentHPOBench 工具和 Codex CLI 之间进行的。这些实验将执行接口的影响与基准任务定义和评分代码隔离开来。完整干预水平的原始结果如表 46-50 所示。有界归一化分数如表 51-55 所示，锚定达到值如表 56-60 所示。The harness ablation keeps the AgentHPOBench task suite, intervention budget, result schema, and scoring pipeline fixed while changing the external execution harness used by the backbone agent. For Claude Sonnet 4.6, the comparison is between the native AgentHPOBench harness and Claude Code CLI. For GPT-5.5, the comparison is between the native AgentHPOBench harness and Codex CLI. These experiments isolate the effect of the execution interface from the benchmark task definitions and scoring code. The complete intervention level raw results are shown in Tables 46–50. The bounded normalized scores are shown in Tables 51–55, and the anchor attainment values are shown in Tables 56–60.
